   165	fn duration_number(e:&Expr)->Option<i64>{
   166	    let Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Number(s),..}=e else{return None;};
   167	    for (suffix,scale) in [("ms",1),("s",1000),("m",60_000),("h",3_600_000),("d",86_400_000)]{
   168	        if let Some(n)=s.strip_suffix(suffix){return n.parse::<i64>().ok()?.checked_mul(scale);}
   169	    }s.parse().ok()
   170	}
   171	fn duration_notes(writes:&[Write<'_>],out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>){
   172	    let mut bounds=BTreeMap::<(String,String,String),BTreeMap<i64,BTreeSet<String>>>::new();
   173	    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
   174	    for w in writes{for condition in conditions(&w.context){walk(condition,|e|{
   175	        let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=e else{return;};if !matches!(op.as_str(),">"|">="|"<"|"<="){return;}
   176	        for (field,clock,cmp) in [(left.as_ref(),right.as_ref(),op.as_str()),(right.as_ref(),left.as_ref(),match op.as_str(){">"=>"<",">="=>"<=","<"=>">",_=>">="})]{
   177	            let Expr::Binary{op:offset,left:now,right:amount,..}=clock else{continue;};
   178	            if !matches!(now.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now")||!matches!(offset.as_str(),"-"|"+"){continue;}
   179	            let Some(mut duration)=duration_number(amount)else{continue;};if offset=="-"{duration=-duration;}
   180	            let target=match field{
   181	                Expr::Member{base,name,..}=>reads(base).into_iter().next().map(|table|(table,name.clone())),
   182	                Expr::Name{name,..}=>writes.iter().filter(|a|a.name==w.name).find_map(|a|a.fields.iter().find(|f|field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Name{name:n,..} if n==name))).map(|f|(a.table.clone(),f.name.clone()))),_=>None,
   183	            };
   184	            if let Some((table,column))=target{let key=(table,column,cmp.to_string());sites.entry(key.clone()).or_insert_with(||w.site.clone());bounds.entry(key).or_default().entry(duration).or_default().insert(w.name.clone());}
   185	        }
   186	    });}}
   187	    for ((table,column,cmp),values) in bounds{if values.len()<2{continue;}
   188	        let entries=values.iter().map(|(ms,ops)|format!("now {ms:+}ms in {}",ops.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ");
   189	        note(out,&sites[&(table.clone(),column.clone(),cmp.clone())],"N_DURATION_BOUNDS",format!("{table}.{column}: inconsistent `{cmp}` duration bounds across operations: {entries}; are these different windows intentional?"),"use the specification's duration consistently at creation, renewal and approval, or document the deliberate workflow difference");
   190	    }
   191	}
   192	
   193	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#169-day-two-precision-and-destructive-evidence-questions — optionality alone is not mandatory evidence.
   194	fn conjuncts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(conjuncts).collect(),_=>vec![rule]}}
   195	fn required_media(bound:&Bound,table:&str,column:&str)->bool{
   196	    fn nonnull(rule:&RuleExpr,column:&str)->bool{
   197	        let pair=match rule{RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>Some((a,b)),RuleExpr::Not(r)=>if let RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)=r.as_ref(){Some((a,b))}else{None},_=>None};
   198	        pair.is_some_and(|(a,b)|[(a,b),(b,a)].iter().any(|(a,b)|matches!(a,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column)&&matches!(b,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))))
   199	    }
   200	    fn mandatory(kind:&ColumnType)->bool{match kind{ColumnType::Image{..}|ColumnType::File{..}|ColumnType::Video{..}=>true,ColumnType::ListRange{of,min,..}=>*min>0&&mandatory(of),_=>false}}
   201	    mandatory(&bound.schema.tables[table].columns[column])||mandatory(bound.schema.tables[table].columns[column].required())&&bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==table&&i.target=="always").filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(conjuncts).any(|r|nonnull(r,column))
   202	}
   203	fn approves(w:&Write<'_>,bound:&Bound)->bool{
   204	    w.fields.iter().any(|f|match field_value(f){
   205	        Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(v),..})=>matches!(bound.schema.tables[&w.table].columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Enum{..}))&&["approved","accepted","authorized","granted","issued","active","confirmed","paid","signed"].contains(&v.as_str()),
   206	        Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Bool(true),..})=>["approv","authoriz"].iter().any(|part|f.name.to_lowercase().contains(part)),_=>false,
   207	    })
   208	}
   209	fn stale_notes(bound:&Bound,writes:&[Write<'_>],out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>){
   210	    let mut groups=BTreeMap::<(String,String),(Site,BTreeSet<String>,BTreeSet<String>,BTreeSet<String>)>::new();
   211	    let indexes=eligibility::indexes(bound);
   212	    for w in writes.iter().filter(|w|matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)){
   213	        let dependencies=eligibility::checks(bound,&indexes,w,w,writes,true);
   214	        for (dep,checks) in dependencies{
   215	            let columns=checks.iter().map(|(c,_)|c.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();for later in writes.iter().filter(|o|o.name!=w.name&&approves(o,bound)&&(o.table==w.table&&o.op==WriteOp::Update||matches!(o.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)&&bound.schema.tables[&o.table].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),ColumnType::Ref{table} if table==&w.table)))){
   216	            let rechecked=eligibility::checks(bound,&indexes,w,later,writes,false);
   217	            if rechecked.get(&dep).is_some_and(|current|checks.is_subset(current)){continue;}
   218	            let g=groups.entry((w.table.clone(),dep.clone())).or_insert_with(||(later.site.clone(),BTreeSet::new(),BTreeSet::new(),BTreeSet::new()));
   219	            if (&later.site.file,later.site.line,later.site.col)<(&g.0.file,g.0.line,g.0.col){g.0=later.site.clone();}
   220	            g.1.insert(format!("{} at {}",w.name,w.site));g.2.insert(format!("{} at {}",later.name,later.site));g.3.extend(columns.clone());
   221	        }}
   222	    }
   223	    for ((table,dep),(site,creators,decisions,columns)) in groups{note(out,&site,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK",format!("{table}: creation by {} checks mutable {dep}.{}, but approval/activation {} does not recheck every column for the same applicant/resource",creators.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "),columns.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(","),decisions.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"decide whether eligibility must remain current at this decision; recheck it or declare independent intent and test create -> eligibility change -> approval");}
   224	}
   225	fn evidence_notes(bound:&Bound,writes:&[Write<'_>],out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>){
   226	    for (child,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
   227	        let media=t.columns.iter().filter(|(c,k)|k.contains_asset()&&required_media(bound,child,c)).map(|(c,_)|c.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();if media.is_empty(){continue;}
   228	        for (reference,kind) in &t.columns{let ColumnType::Ref{table:parent}=kind.required()else{continue;};
   229	            let declared=required_child(bound,parent,child,reference);
   230	            let created_together=writes.iter().any(|w|w.table==*child&&matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)&&writes.iter().any(|p|std::ptr::eq(p.p,w.p)&&p.table==*parent&&matches!(p.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)&&w.fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==reference).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="id"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Write{table,site,..} if table==parent&&site==&p.site))))&&media.iter().any(|c|w.fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==c).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|w.p.args.iter().any(|a|!a.type_.optional&&uses(e,&a.name)))));
   231	            if !declared&&!created_together{continue;}
   232	            let removing=writes.iter().filter(|w|w.table==*child&&(w.op==WriteOp::Delete||matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Update|WriteOp::Upsert)&&w.fields.iter().any(|f|{
   233	                if &f.name==reference{return !field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name==reference&&same_target(base,w)));}
   234	                media.contains(&f.name)&&match field_value(f){Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Null,..})=>true,Some(Expr::Array{items,..})=>items.is_empty(),_=>false}
   235	            }))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   236	            if removing.is_empty(){continue;}
   237	            let operations=removing.iter().map(|w|format!("{} ({})",w.name,if w.op==WriteOp::Delete{"delete"}else{"detach/clear"})).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
   238	            note(out,&removing[0].site,"N_EVIDENCE_REMOVAL",format!("{child}.{reference} -> {parent}: {operations} can remove or move evidence {}; could this remove a parent's last required photo/file? Required child fields alone do not require a surviving child.",media.join(",")),"check the specification for last-child retention; guard deletion/detachment transactionally, replace evidence or retire the parent, then declare independent parent integrity intent and run test");
   239	        }
   240	    }
   241	}
   242	
   243	fn required_child(bound:&Bound,parent:&str,child:&str,reference:&str)->bool{
   244	    bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==parent&&i.target=="always").filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(conjuncts).any(|r|if let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=r{
   245	        table==child&&(bound.schema.tables[table].index(index).is_some_and(|idx|idx.components.iter().zip(key).any(|(c,v)|c.column().map(String::as_str)==Some(reference)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id")))||equalities.iter().any(|(c,v)|c==reference&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id")))
   246	    }else{false})
   247	}
   248	
   249	// The obligation names a relationship and any literal discriminator, not every
   250	// attachment in the child table (e.g. required checkout vs optional return).
   251	fn mandatory_parent_for(bound:&Bound,w:&Write<'_>,writes:&[Write<'_>])->bool{
   252	    fn linked(w:&Write<'_>,reference:&str,parent:&Write<'_>)->bool{w.fields.iter().find(|f|f.name==reference).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="id"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Write{table,site,..} if table==&parent.table&&site==&parent.site)))}
   253	    fn literal(e:&Expr,v:&snapback4_core::Value)->bool{match(e,v){
   254	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(a),..},snapback4_core::Value::String(b))=>a==b,
   255	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Bool(a),..},snapback4_core::Value::Bool(b))=>a==b,
   256	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Null,..},snapback4_core::Value::Null)=>true,
   257	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Number(a),..},snapback4_core::Value::Int(b))=>a.parse::<i64>().ok()==Some(*b),_=>false,
   258	    }}
   259	    for (reference,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&w.table].columns{let ColumnType::Ref{table:parent}=kind.required()else{continue;};
   260	        for p in writes.iter().filter(|p|std::ptr::eq(p.p,w.p)&&p.table==*parent&&matches!(p.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)&&linked(w,reference,p)){
   261	            for policy in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==*parent&&i.target=="always").filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(conjuncts){
   262	                let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=policy else{continue;};if table!=&w.table{continue;}
   263	                let pairs=super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
   264	                if !pairs.iter().any(|(c,v)|c==reference&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id")){continue;}
   265	                let matches=|candidate:&Write<'_>|linked(candidate,reference,p)&&pairs.iter().all(|(column,term)|{
   266	                    if column==reference{return matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id");}
   267	                    let RuleTerm::Literal(value)=term else{return false;};candidate.fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==column).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|literal(e,value))
   268	                });
   269	                if !matches(w){continue;}
   270	                let already=writes.iter().any(|other|std::ptr::eq(other.p,w.p)&&other.table==w.table&&other.site!=w.site&&other.op==WriteOp::Insert&&!other.conditional&&matches(other)&&ordinary_unconditional(other.p,&other.site));
   271	                if !already{return true;}
   272	            }
   273	        }
   274	    }false
   275	}
   276	
   277	fn ordinary_unconditional(p:&ast::ProgramDecl,site:&Site)->bool{
   278	    for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){
   279	        match statement{
   280	            Stmt::Expr{value,..}|Stmt::Bind{value,..}|Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{
   281	                if matches!(value,Expr::Write{op:WriteOp::Insert,site:s,if_absent:false,condition:None,..} if s==site){return true;}
   282	                if matches!(statement,Stmt::Return{..}){return false;}
   283	            },
   284	            Stmt::If{..}|Stmt::For{..}|Stmt::As{..}|Stmt::Break{..}|Stmt::Continue{..}=>return false,_=>{},
   285	        }
   286	    }false
   287	}
   288	
   289	fn asserted_argument(value:&Expr,arg:&str)->bool{
   290	    match value{Expr::Assert{value,..}=>matches!(value.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if name==arg)||asserted_argument(value,arg),_=>false}
   291	}
   292	
   293	fn links_created_parent(w:&Write<'_>,writes:&[&Write<'_>])->bool{
   294	    writes.iter().any(|p|w.fields.iter().any(|f|matches!(field_value(f),Some(Expr::Member{base,name,..}) if name=="id"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Write{table,site,..} if table==&p.table&&site==&p.site))))
   295	}
     1	//! Eligibility suppression binds every checked column to the same created entity.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#169-day-two-precision-and-destructive-evidence-questions — checking the inspector is not checking the applicant.
     3	use super::*;
     4	pub(super) type Indexes=BTreeMap<(String,String),BTreeSet<String>>;
     5	pub(super) fn indexes(bound:&Bound)->Indexes{
     6	    let mut out=Indexes::new();for p in &bound.programs{for r in snapback4_core::ir::read_sites(&p.program){let site=p.sources.get(&r.site).map(|(site,_)|site.to_string()).unwrap_or(r.site);out.entry((r.table,site)).or_default().insert(r.index);}}out
     7	}
     8	fn primary(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,table:&str,site:&Site,keys:&[ast::KeyPart])->bool{
     9	    if keys.len()!=1||keys[0].column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){return false;}
    10	    if keys[0].column.as_deref()==Some("id"){return true;}
    11	    indexes.get(&(table.into(),site.to_string())).is_some_and(|names|names.len()==1&&names.iter().all(|name|bound.schema.tables[table].index(name).is_some_and(|i|i.columns()==["id"])))
    12	}
    13	fn read(e:&Expr)->Option<&ast::ReadExpr>{match e{Expr::Read(r)=>Some(r),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>read(value),_=>None}}
    14	fn id_value(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,e:&Expr,p:&ast::ProgramDecl,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    15	    match e{
    16	        Expr::Name{name,..} if p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name&&matches!(&a.type_.kind,TypeKind::Ref{table:t} if t==table))=>Some(name.clone()),
    17	        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="id"=>{let r=read(base)?;if r.table!=table||!primary(bound,indexes,table,&r.site,&r.keys){return None;}
    18	            if r.keys[0].column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,table)}},
    19	        Expr::Assert{value,..}=>id_value(bound,indexes,value,p,table),_=>None,
    20	    }
    21	}
    22	fn row_id(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,r:&ast::ReadExpr,p:&ast::ProgramDecl)->Option<String>{
    23	    if !primary(bound,indexes,&r.table,&r.site,&r.keys){return None;}if r.keys[0].column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,&r.table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,&r.table)}
    24	}
    25	fn named_id(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,e:&Expr,p:&ast::ProgramDecl,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    26	    match e{Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(_),..}=>Some(crate::print::expression(e)),_=>id_value(bound,indexes,e,p,table)}
    27	}
    28	fn stable_field(bound:&Bound,creator:&Write<'_>,value:&Expr)->Option<String>{
    29	    if !matches!(value,Expr::Literal{..})&&!matches!(value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"||creator.p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name)){return None;}
    30	    creator.fields.iter().filter(|f|bound.schema.tables[&creator.table].immutable_fields().any(|c|c==f.name)).find(|f|field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|crate::print::expression(e)==crate::print::expression(value))).map(|f|f.name.clone())
    31	}
    32	fn normalize(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,e:&Expr,creation:bool)->Option<String>{
    33	    let mut e=e.clone();let origins=if current.table==creator.table&&current.op==WriteOp::Update{
    34	        current.keys.first().filter(|_|primary(bound,indexes,&current.table,&current.site,&current.keys)).and_then(|k|if k.column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&k.value,current.p,&creator.table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&k.value,current.p,&creator.table)}).into_iter().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
    35	    }else{
    36	        current.fields.iter().filter(|f|matches!(bound.schema.tables[&current.table].columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Ref{table}) if table==&creator.table)).map(|f|id_value(bound,indexes,field_value(f)?,current.p,&creator.table)).collect::<Option<BTreeSet<_>>>()?
    37	    };
    38	    if !creation&&origins.len()!=1{return None;}
    39	    super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|{
    40	        let field=if creation{stable_field(bound,creator,e)}else if let Expr::Member{base,name,..}=e{
    41	            read(base).filter(|_|bound.schema.tables[&creator.table].immutable_fields().any(|c|c==name)).filter(|r|r.table==creator.table&&row_id(bound,indexes,r,current.p).is_some_and(|id|origins.contains(&id))).and_then(|_|creator.fields.iter().find(|f|f.name==*name)).and_then(field_value).and_then(|v|stable_field(bound,creator,v))
    42	        }else{None};
    43	        if let Some(field)=field{*e=Expr::Name{name:format!("$created_{field}"),site:e.site().clone()};}
    44	    });
    45	    fn known(e:&Expr)->bool{match e{Expr::Literal{..}=>true,Expr::Name{name,..}=>name.starts_with("$created_"),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>known(value),_=>false}}
    46	    known(&e).then(||crate::print::expression(&e))
    47	}
    48	fn identity(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,r:&ast::ReadExpr,creation:bool)->Option<String>{
    49	    let t=bound.schema.tables.get(&r.table)?;
    50	    let names=indexes.get(&(r.table.clone(),r.site.to_string()))?;if names.len()!=1{return None;}
    51	    let index=t.index(names.first()?)?;if !index.unique||!index.is_plain(){return None;}let columns=index.columns();
    52	    let mut pins=BTreeMap::new();
    53	    for (position,k) in r.keys.iter().enumerate(){let column=k.column.clone().or_else(||columns.get(position).cloned())?;pins.insert(column,normalize(bound,indexes,creator,current,&k.value,creation)?);}
    54	    Some(serde_json::to_string(&columns.iter().map(|c|Some((c,pins.get(c)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?).unwrap())
    55	}
    56	pub(super) fn checks(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,writes:&[Write<'_>],creation:bool)->BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<(String,String)>>{
    57	    let mut out=BTreeMap::<String,BTreeSet<(String,String)>>::new();
    58	    if !creation&&writes.iter().any(|w|std::ptr::eq(w.p,current.p)&&w.table==creator.table&&matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert|WriteOp::Delete)){return out;}
    59	
    60	    let mutable=|table:&str,column:&str|bound.schema.tables.get(table).is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(column)&&!t.immutable_fields().any(|c|c==column))&&writes.iter().any(|o|o.table==table&&(o.op==WriteOp::Delete||matches!(o.op,WriteOp::Update|WriteOp::Upsert)&&o.fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column)));
    61	    for condition in conditions(&current.context){walk(condition,|e|{
    62	        let (r,columns)=match e{
    63	            Expr::Member{base,name,..}=>{let Some(r)=read(base)else{return;};(r,vec![name.clone()])},
    64	            Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=>{let mut cols=r.keys.iter().filter_map(|k|k.column.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();if writes.iter().any(|o|o.table==r.table&&o.op==WriteOp::Delete){cols.push("existence".into());}(r,cols)},_=>return,
    65	        };
    66	        if r.table==creator.table||!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&r.table).is_empty(){return;}
    67	        let entity=match identity(bound,indexes,creator,current,r,creation){Some(entity)=>entity,None if creation=>format!("$unknown:{}",crate::print::expression(&Expr::Read(r.clone()))),None=>return};
    68	        for column in columns{if column=="existence"||mutable(&r.table,&column){out.entry(r.table.clone()).or_default().insert((column,entity.clone()));}}
    69	    });}out
    70	}
//! Creation is a write even when an edit operation has the stronger guard.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#151-every-executed-write — conditional advice never infers creation policy.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|crate::parse_document(file,text).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let catalog=Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
    let mut writes=Vec::new();
    for doc in &documents{for p in &doc.programs{if p.kind!=ast::ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
        for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
            let ast::Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=write else{continue;};
            let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&table)else{continue;};
            let requirements=super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context);
            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
            let gate=if op==ast::WriteOp::Update{t.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else{t.rules.insert.clone()};
            let admission=RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([gate]).collect());
            writes.push((p,p.name.clone(),op,table,fields,site,admission));
        }
    }}
    fn necessary(rule:&RuleExpr)->RuleExpr{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(necessary).collect()),
        _ if super::operation::safe(rule)=>rule.clone(),_=>RuleExpr::Allow,
    }}
    // Recover only column-specific conditional guards; ordinary table-wide
    // differences keep their existing inventory code and priority.
    let mut specific=BTreeMap::<(String,String,String,String),(bool,Vec<RuleExpr>)>::new();
    for doc in &documents{for p in &doc.programs{if p.kind!=ast::ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
        let plain=super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p);
        let columns=plain.iter().filter_map(|(e,_)|if let ast::Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Update,fields,..}=e{Some(fields.iter().map(|f|f.name.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>())}else{None}).flatten().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        for column in columns{for ((write,context),(_,base)) in super::authority_walk::column_writes(&catalog,p,&column).into_iter().zip(&plain){
            let ast::Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Update,table,fields,..}=write else{continue;};
            if !fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column){continue;}
            if plain.iter().filter(|(e,_)|matches!(e,ast::Expr::Write{table:t,..} if t==&table)).count()!=1{continue;}
            let requirements=super::operation::requirements(&catalog,&catalog.tables[&table],&context);
            let restriction=RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([bound.schema.tables[&table].update_rule_judged().into_owned()]).collect());
            let entry=specific.entry((p.site.file.clone(),p.name.clone(),table,column.clone())).or_default();entry.0|=context.body.len()>base.body.len();entry.1.push(necessary(&restriction));
        }}
    }}
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut replaced=BTreeSet::new();
    for (creator,name,op,table,fields,site,admission) in &writes{
        if !matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert){continue;}
        for ((file,editor,target,column),(conditional,guards)) in &specific{
            if !conditional{continue;}
            let guard=RuleExpr::Or(guards.clone());
            if target!=table||!fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column&&supplied(f,creator)){continue;}
            let guard=necessary(&guard);
            let module=std::path::Path::new(file).file_stem().and_then(|s|s.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
            let operation=super::super::operation_name(module,editor);
            let Some(program)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==operation)else{continue;};
            let dependencies=kernel::dependencies(&guard);
            if snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&program.program).iter().any(|(table,_)|dependencies.contains(table)){continue;}
            if kernel::implies(admission,&guard)!=Ok(false)||!seen.insert((creator.site.file.clone(),name.clone(),table.clone(),BTreeSet::from([column.clone()]),guard.quarry())){continue;}
            let audience=bound.schema.tables[table].column_reads.get(column);
            let private=audience.is_some_and(|r|kernel::implies(admission,r)==Ok(false));
            let code=if private{"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"}else{"N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"};
            let detail=if private{format!("; write-only private payload: creator admission does not imply column read audience {}",audience.unwrap().quarry())}else{String::new()};
            out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,format!("{name}: argument-supplied {table}.{column} is open at create/upsert, but {operation} requires {} when this column changes{detail}",guard.quarry()),"confirm this column's creation authority against the specification; apply its change guard at creation too when required, declare independent column write intent, then run test with a non-null value").guide("security"));
            replaced.insert((creator.site.file.clone(),name.clone(),table.clone(),column.clone()));
        }
    }
    seen.clear();
    for (creator,name,op,table,fields,site,admission) in &writes{
        if !matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert){continue;}
        for (_,editor,edit,other,patch,_,restricted) in &writes{
            if *edit!=ast::WriteOp::Update||other!=table{continue;}
            let restriction=necessary(restricted);
            if kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&restriction)==Ok(true)||kernel::implies(admission,&restriction)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let columns=patch.iter().filter(|f|!replaced.contains(&(creator.site.file.clone(),name.clone(),table.clone(),f.name.clone()))&&fields.iter().any(|insert|insert.name==f.name&&supplied(insert,creator))).map(|f|f.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            if columns.is_empty()||!seen.insert((creator.site.file.clone(),name.clone(),table.clone(),columns.clone(),restriction.quarry())){continue;}
            out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,"N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT",format!("{name}: {table}.{} can be supplied at insert/upsert without the edit restriction of {editor}: {}; creation admission: {}",columns.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(","),restriction.quarry(),admission.quarry()),"confirm whether the spec permits different creation authority; otherwise guard every insert/upsert arm too and declare column write only intent, then run snapback4 test").guide("security"));
        }
    }out
}

fn supplied(field:&ast::ObjectField,program:&ast::ProgramDecl)->bool{
    let Some(value)=&field.value else{return true;};let mut value=value.clone();let mut supplied=false;
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |e|if let ast::Expr::Name{name,..}=e{supplied|=program.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name);});
    supplied
}
//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
mod changes;
use changes::Change;
fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
    let mut expr=expr.clone();
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
    });expr
}
fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],changes:&[Change],column:Option<&str>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
    let changes=if column.is_some()&&changes::prior_effects(&expr){&[][..]}else{changes};
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();if let Some(column)=column{for change in changes{if change.matches(e,column,p){context.body.extend(change.requirements.clone());}}}out.push((e.clone(),context));},
        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
            match &f.body{
                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,changes,column,depth+1,out),
                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,&mut changes.to_vec(),column,depth+1,out),
            }
        },_=>{}
    });
}
// Only an unconditional direct call exports a helper's guaranteed prefix.
// Conditional/loop-local requirements never become caller assumptions.
fn helper_guards(catalog:&Catalog,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,depth:usize)->Vec<Stmt>{
    if depth>=32{return Vec::new();}
    let Expr::Call{name,args,..}=resolve(expr,env)else{return Vec::new();};
    let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(&name)else{return Vec::new();};
    let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(a,v)|(a.name.clone(),v)).collect::<Env>();
    let FunctionBody::Block(body)=&f.body else{return if let FunctionBody::Expression(value)=&f.body{helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)}else{Vec::new()};};
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&locals),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));if names.len()==1{locals.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&locals));}},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)),
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));break;},_=>break,
    }}out
}
fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,changes:&mut Vec<Change>,column:Option<&str>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    for s in body{
        let consuming=column.is_some()&&changes::effectful(s);
        match s{
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);
            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
        },
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);break;},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));},
        Stmt::Emit{payload,once,..}=>{
            if column.is_some(){changes.clear();}
            expression(catalog,p,payload,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);
            if let Some(once)=once{expression(catalog,p,once,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);}
        },
        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,site}=>{
            if column.is_some()&&changes::expression_effectful(&resolve(condition,env)){changes.clear();}
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);
            let condition=resolve(condition,env);
            let mut then_guards=guards.clone();
            then_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:condition.clone(),code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut then_guards,&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out);
            let mut else_guards=guards.clone();
            else_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:Expr::Unary{op:"not".into(),value:Box::new(condition.clone()),site:site.clone()},code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut else_guards,&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out);
            let assigned=branch_assignments(then_body).into_iter().chain(branch_assignments(else_body)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            if column.is_some()&&assigned.is_empty(){changes.extend(changes::after(catalog,&condition,then_body,else_body,env));}
            for name in assigned{env.insert(name.clone(),Expr::Name{name:format!("$advice_unknown_{name}"),site:site.clone()});}
        },
        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,site}=>{if column.is_some(){changes.clear();}expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),&mut Vec::new(),column,depth,out);for name in branch_assignments(body){env.insert(name.clone(),Expr::Name{name:format!("$advice_unknown_{name}"),site:site.clone()});}},
        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out),_=>{}
    }
    if consuming{changes.clear();}
    }
}
pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{walk_writes(catalog,p,None)}
pub(super) fn column_writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,column:&str)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{walk_writes(catalog,p,Some(column))}
fn walk_writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,column:Option<&str>)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();let mut changes=Vec::new();
    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,&mut changes,column,0,&mut out);
    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,&mut changes,column,0,&mut out);out
}

fn branch_assignments(body:&[Stmt])->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::new();for s in body{match s{Stmt::Bind{names,..}=>out.extend(names.clone()),Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.extend(branch_assignments(then_body));out.extend(branch_assignments(else_body));},Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>out.extend(branch_assignments(body)),_=>{}}}out
}
//! Changed-column guards are advisory and bind the exact row, field and new value.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#169-day-two-precision-and-destructive-evidence-questions — no table-wide promotion of a column condition.
use super::*;
#[derive(Clone)]
pub(super) struct Change{old:Expr,new:Expr,pub requirements:Vec<Stmt>}
fn read(e:&Expr)->Option<&ast::ReadExpr>{match e{Expr::Read(r)=>Some(r),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>read(value),_=>None}}
impl Change{
    pub fn matches(&self,write:&Expr,column:&str,p:&ProgramDecl)->bool{
        let Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Update,table,keys,fields,..}=write else{return false;};
        let Expr::Member{base,name,..}=&self.old else{return false;};let Some(old)=read(base)else{return false;};
        fn stable(e:&Expr,p:&ProgramDecl)->bool{match e{Expr::Literal{..}=>true,Expr::Name{name,..}=>name=="viewer"||p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>stable(value,p),_=>false}}
        old.keys.iter().all(|k|stable(&k.value,p))&&keys.iter().all(|k|stable(&k.value,p))&&name==column&&old.table==*table&&old.keys.len()==keys.len()&&old.keys.iter().zip(keys).all(|(a,b)|a.column==b.column&&crate::print::expression(&a.value)==crate::print::expression(&b.value))&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column&&f.value.as_ref().is_some_and(|v|crate::print::expression(v)==crate::print::expression(&self.new)))
    }
}
// Unknown calls and writes end the pending interval; pure leading helper
// requirements can be recovered only inside the immediately tested branch.
pub(super) fn expression_effectful(e:&Expr)->bool{
    let mut e=e.clone();let mut unknown=false;super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Write{..}|Expr::Call{..}){unknown=true;});unknown
}
pub(super) fn prior_effects(e:&Expr)->bool{
    let mut e=e.clone();let(mut calls,mut writes)=(false,0);
    super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|match e{Expr::Write{..}=>writes+=1,Expr::Call{..}=>calls=true,_=>{}});
    calls||writes>1
}
pub(super) fn effectful(s:&Stmt)->bool{match s{
    Stmt::Expr{value,..}|Stmt::Bind{value,..}|Stmt::Return{value,..}|Stmt::Require{condition:value,..}=>expression_effectful(value),
    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>expression_effectful(condition)||then_body.iter().chain(else_body).any(effectful),
    Stmt::For{..}|Stmt::As{..}|Stmt::Emit{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}

fn prefix(_catalog:&Catalog,body:&[Stmt],env:&Env)->Vec<Stmt>{
    let mut env=env.clone();let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{if effectful(s){break;}if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&env));}},
        _=>break,
    }}out
}
pub(super) fn after(catalog:&Catalog,condition:&Expr,then_body:&[Stmt],else_body:&[Stmt],env:&Env)->Vec<Change>{
    if expression_effectful(condition){return Vec::new();}
    let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=condition else{return Vec::new();};
    let body=match op.as_str(){"!="=>then_body,"="=>else_body,_=>return Vec::new()};
    if body.iter().any(|s|matches!(s,Stmt::Return{..})||effectful(s)){return Vec::new();}
    let requirements=prefix(catalog,body,env);if requirements.is_empty(){return Vec::new();}
    [(left,right),(right,left)].into_iter().filter(|(old,_)|matches!(old.as_ref(),Expr::Member{..})).map(|(old,new)|Change{old:*old.clone(),new:*new.clone(),requirements:requirements.clone()}).collect()
}

   232	            let removing=writes.iter().filter(|w|w.table==*child&&(w.op==WriteOp::Delete||matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Update|WriteOp::Upsert)&&w.fields.iter().any(|f|{
   233	                if &f.name==reference{return !field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name==reference&&same_target(base,w)));}
   234	                media.contains(&f.name)&&match field_value(f){Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Null,..})=>true,Some(Expr::Array{items,..})=>items.is_empty(),_=>false}
   235	            }))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   236	            if removing.is_empty(){continue;}
   237	            let operations=removing.iter().map(|w|format!("{} ({})",w.name,if w.op==WriteOp::Delete{"delete"}else{"detach/clear"})).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
   238	            note(out,&removing[0].site,"N_EVIDENCE_REMOVAL",format!("{child}.{reference} -> {parent}: {operations} can remove or move evidence {}; could this remove a parent's last required photo/file? Required child fields alone do not require a surviving child.",media.join(",")),"check the specification for last-child retention; guard deletion/detachment transactionally, replace evidence or retire the parent, then declare independent parent integrity intent and run test");
   239	        }
   240	    }
   241	}
   242	
   243	fn required_child(bound:&Bound,parent:&str,child:&str,reference:&str)->bool{
   244	    bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==parent&&i.target=="always").filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(conjuncts).any(|r|if let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=r{
   245	        table==child&&(bound.schema.tables[table].index(index).is_some_and(|idx|idx.components.iter().zip(key).any(|(c,v)|c.column().map(String::as_str)==Some(reference)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id")))||equalities.iter().any(|(c,v)|c==reference&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id")))
   246	    }else{false})
   247	}
   248	
   249	// The obligation names a relationship and any literal discriminator, not every
   250	// attachment in the child table (e.g. required checkout vs optional return).
   251	fn mandatory_parent_for(bound:&Bound,w:&Write<'_>,writes:&[Write<'_>])->bool{
   252	    fn linked(w:&Write<'_>,reference:&str,parent:&Write<'_>)->bool{w.fields.iter().find(|f|f.name==reference).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="id"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Write{table,site,..} if table==&parent.table&&site==&parent.site)))}
   253	    fn literal(e:&Expr,v:&snapback4_core::Value)->bool{match(e,v){
   254	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(a),..},snapback4_core::Value::String(b))=>a==b,
   255	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Bool(a),..},snapback4_core::Value::Bool(b))=>a==b,
   256	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Null,..},snapback4_core::Value::Null)=>true,
   257	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Number(a),..},snapback4_core::Value::Int(b))=>a.parse::<i64>().ok()==Some(*b),_=>false,
   258	    }}
   259	    for (reference,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&w.table].columns{let ColumnType::Ref{table:parent}=kind.required()else{continue;};
   260	        for p in writes.iter().filter(|p|std::ptr::eq(p.p,w.p)&&p.table==*parent&&matches!(p.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)&&linked(w,reference,p)){
   261	            for policy in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==*parent&&i.target=="always").filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(conjuncts){
   262	                let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=policy else{continue;};if table!=&w.table{continue;}
   263	                let pairs=super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
   264	                if !pairs.iter().any(|(c,v)|c==reference&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id")){continue;}
   265	                let matches=|candidate:&Write<'_>|linked(candidate,reference,p)&&pairs.iter().all(|(column,term)|{
   266	                    if column==reference{return matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id");}
   267	                    let RuleTerm::Literal(value)=term else{return false;};candidate.fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==column).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|literal(e,value))
   268	                });
   269	                if !matches(w){continue;}
   270	                let already=writes.iter().any(|other|std::ptr::eq(other.p,w.p)&&other.table==w.table&&other.site!=w.site&&other.op==WriteOp::Insert&&!other.conditional&&matches(other)&&ordinary_unconditional(other.p,&other.site));
   271	                if !already{return true;}
   272	            }
   273	        }
   274	    }false
   275	}
   276	
   277	fn ordinary_unconditional(p:&ast::ProgramDecl,site:&Site)->bool{
   278	    for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){
   279	        match statement{
   280	            Stmt::Expr{value,..}|Stmt::Bind{value,..}|Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{
   281	                if matches!(value,Expr::Write{op:WriteOp::Insert,site:s,if_absent:false,condition:None,..} if s==site){return true;}
   282	                if matches!(statement,Stmt::Return{..}){return false;}
   283	            },
   284	            Stmt::If{..}|Stmt::For{..}|Stmt::As{..}|Stmt::Break{..}|Stmt::Continue{..}=>return false,_=>{},
   285	        }
   286	    }false
   287	}

   289	fn asserted_argument(value:&Expr,arg:&str)->bool{
   290	    match value{Expr::Assert{value,..}=>matches!(value.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if name==arg)||asserted_argument(value,arg),_=>false}
   291	}

   293	fn links_created_parent(w:&Write<'_>,writes:&[&Write<'_>])->bool{
   294	    writes.iter().any(|p|w.fields.iter().any(|f|matches!(field_value(f),Some(Expr::Member{base,name,..}) if name=="id"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Write{table,site,..} if table==&p.table&&site==&p.site))))
   295	}
     1	//! Eligibility suppression binds every checked column to the same created entity.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#169-day-two-precision-and-destructive-evidence-questions — checking the inspector is not checking the applicant.
     3	use super::*;
     4	pub(super) type Indexes=BTreeMap<(String,String),BTreeSet<String>>;
     5	pub(super) fn indexes(bound:&Bound)->Indexes{
     6	    let mut out=Indexes::new();for p in &bound.programs{for r in snapback4_core::ir::read_sites(&p.program){let site=p.sources.get(&r.site).map(|(site,_)|site.to_string()).unwrap_or(r.site);out.entry((r.table,site)).or_default().insert(r.index);}}out
     7	}
     8	fn primary(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,table:&str,site:&Site,keys:&[ast::KeyPart])->bool{
     9	    if keys.len()!=1||keys[0].column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){return false;}
    10	    if keys[0].column.as_deref()==Some("id"){return true;}
    11	    indexes.get(&(table.into(),site.to_string())).is_some_and(|names|names.len()==1&&names.iter().all(|name|bound.schema.tables[table].index(name).is_some_and(|i|i.columns()==["id"])))
    12	}
    13	fn read(e:&Expr)->Option<&ast::ReadExpr>{match e{Expr::Read(r)=>Some(r),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>read(value),_=>None}}
    14	fn id_value(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,e:&Expr,p:&ast::ProgramDecl,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    15	    match e{
    16	        Expr::Name{name,..} if p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name&&matches!(&a.type_.kind,TypeKind::Ref{table:t} if t==table))=>Some(name.clone()),
    17	        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="id"=>{let r=read(base)?;if r.table!=table||!primary(bound,indexes,table,&r.site,&r.keys){return None;}
    18	            if r.keys[0].column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,table)}},
    19	        Expr::Assert{value,..}=>id_value(bound,indexes,value,p,table),_=>None,
    20	    }
    21	}
    22	fn row_id(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,r:&ast::ReadExpr,p:&ast::ProgramDecl)->Option<String>{
    23	    if !primary(bound,indexes,&r.table,&r.site,&r.keys){return None;}if r.keys[0].column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,&r.table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,&r.table)}
    24	}
    25	fn named_id(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,e:&Expr,p:&ast::ProgramDecl,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    26	    match e{Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(_),..}=>Some(crate::print::expression(e)),_=>id_value(bound,indexes,e,p,table)}
    27	}
    28	fn stable_field(bound:&Bound,creator:&Write<'_>,value:&Expr)->Option<String>{
    29	    if !matches!(value,Expr::Literal{..})&&!matches!(value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"||creator.p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name)){return None;}
    30	    creator.fields.iter().filter(|f|bound.schema.tables[&creator.table].immutable_fields().any(|c|c==f.name)).find(|f|field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|crate::print::expression(e)==crate::print::expression(value))).map(|f|f.name.clone())
    31	}
    32	fn normalize(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,e:&Expr,creation:bool)->Option<String>{
    33	    let mut e=e.clone();let origins=if current.table==creator.table&&current.op==WriteOp::Update{
    34	        current.keys.first().filter(|_|primary(bound,indexes,&current.table,&current.site,&current.keys)).and_then(|k|if k.column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&k.value,current.p,&creator.table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&k.value,current.p,&creator.table)}).into_iter().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
    35	    }else{
    36	        current.fields.iter().filter(|f|matches!(bound.schema.tables[&current.table].columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Ref{table}) if table==&creator.table)).map(|f|id_value(bound,indexes,field_value(f)?,current.p,&creator.table)).collect::<Option<BTreeSet<_>>>()?
    37	    };
    38	    if !creation&&origins.len()!=1{return None;}
    39	    super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|{
    40	        let field=if creation{stable_field(bound,creator,e)}else if let Expr::Member{base,name,..}=e{
    41	            read(base).filter(|_|bound.schema.tables[&creator.table].immutable_fields().any(|c|c==name)).filter(|r|r.table==creator.table&&row_id(bound,indexes,r,current.p).is_some_and(|id|origins.contains(&id))).and_then(|_|creator.fields.iter().find(|f|f.name==*name)).and_then(field_value).and_then(|v|stable_field(bound,creator,v))
    42	        }else{None};
    43	        if let Some(field)=field{*e=Expr::Name{name:format!("$created_{field}"),site:e.site().clone()};}
    44	    });
    45	    fn known(e:&Expr)->bool{match e{Expr::Literal{..}=>true,Expr::Name{name,..}=>name.starts_with("$created_"),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>known(value),_=>false}}
    46	    known(&e).then(||crate::print::expression(&e))
    47	}
    48	fn identity(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,r:&ast::ReadExpr,creation:bool)->Option<String>{
    49	    let t=bound.schema.tables.get(&r.table)?;
    50	    let names=indexes.get(&(r.table.clone(),r.site.to_string()))?;if names.len()!=1{return None;}
    51	    let index=t.index(names.first()?)?;if !index.unique||!index.is_plain(){return None;}let columns=index.columns();
    52	    let mut pins=BTreeMap::new();
    53	    for (position,k) in r.keys.iter().enumerate(){let column=k.column.clone().or_else(||columns.get(position).cloned())?;pins.insert(column,normalize(bound,indexes,creator,current,&k.value,creation)?);}
    54	    Some(serde_json::to_string(&columns.iter().map(|c|Some((c,pins.get(c)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?).unwrap())
    55	}
    56	pub(super) fn checks(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,writes:&[Write<'_>],creation:bool)->BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<(String,String)>>{
    57	    let mut out=BTreeMap::<String,BTreeSet<(String,String)>>::new();
    58	    if !creation&&writes.iter().any(|w|std::ptr::eq(w.p,current.p)&&w.table==creator.table&&matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert|WriteOp::Delete)){return out;}
    59	
    60	    let mutable=|table:&str,column:&str|bound.schema.tables.get(table).is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(column)&&!t.immutable_fields().any(|c|c==column))&&writes.iter().any(|o|o.table==table&&(o.op==WriteOp::Delete||matches!(o.op,WriteOp::Update|WriteOp::Upsert)&&o.fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column)));
    61	    for condition in conditions(&current.context){walk(condition,|e|{
    62	        let (r,columns)=match e{
    63	            Expr::Member{base,name,..}=>{let Some(r)=read(base)else{return;};(r,vec![name.clone()])},
    64	            Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=>{let mut cols=r.keys.iter().filter_map(|k|k.column.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();if writes.iter().any(|o|o.table==r.table&&o.op==WriteOp::Delete){cols.push("existence".into());}(r,cols)},_=>return,
    65	        };
    66	        if r.table==creator.table||!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&r.table).is_empty(){return;}
    67	        let entity=match identity(bound,indexes,creator,current,r,creation){Some(entity)=>entity,None if creation=>format!("$unknown:{}",crate::print::expression(&Expr::Read(r.clone()))),None=>return};
    68	        for column in columns{if column=="existence"||mutable(&r.table,&column){out.entry(r.table.clone()).or_default().insert((column,entity.clone()));}}
    69	    });}out
    70	}

 let fixed=s.replace("  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS","  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS\n  require not exists operatorFlags[app.owner, suspended = true] else SUSPENDED");assert!(!has(&fixed,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK"));
}
#[test]fn stale_eligibility_rechecks_bind_the_applicant_and_every_mutable_column(){
 let s=include_str!("security/wave23/030.q");
 let wrong=s.replace("  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS","  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS\n  require not exists operatorFlags[viewer, suspended = true] else SUSPENDED");assert!(has(&wrong,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK"));
 let dual=s.replace("  suspended: bool", "  disabled: bool default false\n  suspended: bool").replace("flag.suspended = false", "(flag.suspended = false and flag.disabled = false)").replace("suspended: true }", "suspended: true, disabled:true }");
 let partial=dual.replace("  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS","  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS\n  require not exists operatorFlags[app.owner, suspended = true] else SUSPENDED");assert!(has(&partial,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK"));
 let complete=partial.replace("  require not exists operatorFlags[app.owner, suspended = true] else SUSPENDED","  require not exists operatorFlags[app.owner, suspended = true] else SUSPENDED\n  require not exists operatorFlags[app.owner, disabled = true] else DISABLED");assert!(!has(&complete,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK"));
}

 let alias=format!("{alias}\nmutation transferEligibility(application:applications, operator:principal):\n  update applications[application] {{eligibilityOwner:operator}}\n");assert!(has(&alias,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK"));
}
#[test]fn mutable_named_id_selectors_cannot_equal_a_previously_captured_row_by_spelling(){
 let s="table flags:\n  owner:principal\n  suspended:bool\n  unique owner\n  immutable owner\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\ntable apps:\n  owner:principal\n  status:enum('pending','approved')\n  immutable owner\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\ntable selectors:\n  app:apps\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation create():\n  f = flags[viewer]\n  require f = null or f.suspended = false else SUSPENDED\n  return insert apps {owner:viewer,status:'pending'}\nmutation flag(owner:principal,suspended:bool):\n  upsert flags[owner] {owner,suspended}\nmutation approve(selector:selectors,other:apps):\n  old = selectors[selector] ! else MISSING\n  app = apps[id = old.app] ! else MISSING\n  f = flags[app.owner]\n  require f = null or f.suspended = false else SUSPENDED\n  update selectors[selector] {app:other}\n  fresh = selectors[selector] ! else MISSING\n  update apps[id = fresh.app] {status:'approved'}\n";
 assert!(has(s,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK"));
}
#[test]fn named_non_id_reference_indexes_do_not_select_the_argument_row(){

 assert!(has(s,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK"));
 let through_id=s.replace("  update apps[id = application]", "  original = apps[id = application] ! else MISSING\n  update apps[id = original.id]");assert!(has(&through_id,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK"));

 assert!(c.violations.iter().any(|v|v.reachability.is_reachable()&&v.operation=="createSigning"&&v.history.last().and_then(|s|s.args.get("agencyBillingNotes")).is_some_and(|v|*v!=snapback4_core::Value::Null)&&v.row.field("agencyBillingNotes").is_ok_and(|v|*v!=snapback4_core::Value::Null)),"no non-null replay among {} witnesses",c.violations.len());
 assert!(c.directed_attempts<=2048&&c.directed_peak_worlds<=16);

#[test]fn changed_field_guards_survive_a_helper_and_lexical_aliases(){
 let s="table roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('owner','writer')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ntable docs:\n  secret: text <=100 ? read <- exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner']\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(secret:text <=100 ?):\n  return insert docs {secret}\nfn editHelper(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  old = docs[doc] ! else MISSING\n  next = secret\n  if next != old.secret:\n    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  update docs[doc] {secret:next}\n  return null\nmutation edit(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  return editHelper(doc,secret)\n";
 assert!(has(s,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let equality=s.replace("if next != old.secret:\n    require", "if next = old.secret:\n    require true else SAME\n  else:\n    require");assert!(has(&equality,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let reassigned=s.replace("    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  update", "    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  else:\n    next = 'changed'\n  update");assert!(!has(&reassigned,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let multiple=s.replace("  if next != old.secret:", "  update docs[doc] {secret:'intervening'}\n  if next != old.secret:");assert!(!has(&multiple,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
}
#[test]fn effectful_loop_domains_never_reuse_a_preloop_column_guard(){

 let rejected=compile(&[("schema.q".into(),s.into())]);assert!(rejected.diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.code=="E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT"),"the original helper-list counterexample is already outside the language");

}

#[test]fn one_mutation_keeps_independent_upload_sites(){

}

#[test]fn one_upload_argument_at_one_site_keeps_two_column_obligations(){

}
//! Individually justified source-bound dispositions; never proof or authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#162-narrow-gates-and-individual-dispositions — bulk compiler-note review cannot clear security gates.
use super::*;
use serde::{Serialize,Deserialize};
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
const ANALYZER:&str="sec7-7";
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct File{version:u32,entries:BTreeMap<String,Entry>}
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Entry{sources:String,analyzer:String,reason:String}
#[derive(Serialize)]
pub(super) struct Disposition{pub id:String,pub code:String,pub site:String,pub gated:bool,pub acknowledged:bool,pub reason:Option<String>}
pub(super) fn gated(code:&str)->bool{matches!(code,"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT"|"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE")}
fn hash(bytes:&[u8])->String{format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(bytes))}
fn digest(s:&str)->bool{s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_hexdigit())}
fn id(n:&snapback4_lang::Diagnostic)->String{hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::to_value(n).unwrap()).unwrap())}
pub(super) fn review(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>)->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    if project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed during security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut sorted=sources.to_vec();sorted.sort_by(|a,b|a.0.cmp(&b.0));
    let fingerprint=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&sorted).unwrap());
    let path=project.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");
    // Keep the lock inode in ignored generated output. Do not unlink a lock
    // while another process can be waiting on that inode. Kernel ownership
    // releases it on exit, including an interrupted writer.
    let _writer_lock=if ack.is_some(){
        let directory=project.schema_dir().join("generated");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&directory).map_err(|e|format!("cannot create security review lock directory: {e}"))?;
        let lock=std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).truncate(false).read(true).write(true).open(directory.join("security-reviews.lock")).map_err(|e|format!("cannot open security review lock: {e}"))?;
        lock.lock().map_err(|e|format!("cannot acquire security review lock: {e}"))?;Some(lock)
    }else{None};
    if project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed while waiting for security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut file:File=match std::fs::read(&path){
        Ok(bytes)=>serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_|"invalid snapback/security-reviews.json; expected version 1 with per-note source/analyzer/reason records".to_string())?,
        Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>File{version:1,entries:BTreeMap::new()},
        Err(e)=>return Err(format!("cannot read security dispositions: {e}")),
    };
    if file.version!=1||file.entries.iter().any(|(key,e)|!digest(key)||!digest(&e.sources)||e.reason.trim().is_empty()||e.analyzer.is_empty()){return Err("invalid security disposition version/digest/reason".into());}
    if let Some((key,reason))=ack{
        if reason.trim().is_empty(){return Err("--reason needs the specification rationale for this individual note".into());}
        if !notes.iter().any(|n|id(n)==key){return Err("--ack-security ID is not a current security note; run check --json".into());}
        file.entries.insert(key.into(),Entry{sources:fingerprint.clone(),analyzer:ANALYZER.into(),reason:reason.trim().into()});
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("json.{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let result=std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&file).unwrap()).and_then(|_|{
            if project.sources()?!=sources{return Err(std::io::Error::other("Quarry sources changed before disposition publication; rerun check"));}
            std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path)
        });
        if let Err(e)=result{let _=std::fs::remove_file(&temporary);return Err(format!("cannot record security disposition: {e}"));}
    }
    Ok(notes.iter().map(|n|{let key=id(n);let entry=file.entries.get(&key).filter(|e|e.sources==fingerprint&&e.analyzer==ANALYZER);
        Disposition{id:key,code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated:gated(&n.code),acknowledged:entry.is_some(),reason:entry.map(|e|e.reason.clone())}
    }).collect())
}
pub(super) fn pending(dispositions:&[Disposition])->bool{dispositions.iter().any(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged)}
pub(super) fn print(dispositions:&[Disposition]){for d in dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.gated||d.acknowledged){
    if d.acknowledged{println!("security reviewed {} {}: {}",d.id,d.code,d.reason.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));}
    else{println!("security review required {} {} at {}; repair or check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'",d.id,d.code,d.site,d.id);}
}}

struct Scratch(PathBuf);impl Drop for Scratch{fn drop(&mut self){let _=fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
fn scratch()->Scratch{let p=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-sec7-cli-{}-{}",std::process::id(),{static NEXT:std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize=std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize::new(0);NEXT.fetch_add(1,std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)}));fs::create_dir_all(p.join("snapback")).unwrap();Scratch(p)}
const SOURCE:&str="table docs:\n  photo: image <=10000 ?\n  other: image <=10000 ?\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- deny\n  online only\ninvariant docs always .photo != null and .other != null\nmutation add(photo: image <=10000 ?, other: image <=10000 ?):\n  return insert docs {photo,other}\n";
fn check(p:&Scratch,args:&[&str])->(i32,Value){let r=Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4")).args(["check","--json"]).args(args).current_dir(&p.0).output().unwrap();let j=serde_json::from_slice(&r.stdout).unwrap_or_else(|_|panic!("{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&r.stdout)));(r.status.code().unwrap_or(-1),j)}
#[test]fn one_note_at_a_time_bulk_cannot_clear_and_source_edits_invalidate(){
 let p=scratch();let source=p.0.join("snapback/schema.q");fs::write(&source,SOURCE).unwrap();
 let (code,j)=check(&p,&[]);assert_eq!(code,1);assert_eq!(j["ok"],false);assert_eq!(j["security"]["invariants"].as_array().unwrap().len(),1,"explicit mandatory-media intent supplies the gate evidence");
 assert_eq!(j["securityReviews"],check(&p,&[]).1["securityReviews"],"same input must produce stable IDs");
 let pending=j["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|d|d["gated"]==true).map(|d|d["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>();assert_eq!(pending.len(),2);
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-notes"]).0,1);assert!(!p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json").exists());
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[0],"--reason","reviewed unreachable draft creation helper"]).0,1);
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[1],"--reason","reviewed unreachable second draft creation helper"]).0,0);
 let review=p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json");let bytes=fs::read(&review).unwrap();
 fs::write(&source,format!("{SOURCE}\n-- source changed\n")).unwrap();assert_eq!(check(&p,&[]).0,1);
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[0],"--reason","  "]).0,2);assert_eq!(fs::read(&review).unwrap(),bytes);
 std::thread::scope(|scope|{
  let a=scope.spawn(||check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[0],"--reason","optional first photo after source review"]));
  let b=scope.spawn(||check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[1],"--reason","optional second photo after source review"]));
  assert!([0,1].contains(&a.join().unwrap().0));assert!([0,1].contains(&b.join().unwrap().0));
 });
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&[]).0,0,"concurrent dispositions must both survive");
 let bytes=fs::read(&review).unwrap();
 fs::write(&source,"not valid Quarry\n").unwrap();assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[0],"--reason","cannot override compile error"]).0,1);assert_eq!(fs::read(review).unwrap(),bytes);
}

#[test]fn one_upload_argument_at_two_creation_sites_needs_two_individual_reviews(){
 let p=scratch();let source="table first:\n  photo:image <=10000 ?\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- deny\n  online only\ntable second:\n  photo:image <=10000 ?\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- deny\n  online only\ninvariant first always .photo != null\ninvariant second always .photo != null\nmutation add(photo:image <=10000 ?):\n  insert first {photo}\n  insert second {photo}\n  return null\n";fs::write(p.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),source).unwrap();
 let (code,j)=check(&p,&[]);assert_eq!(code,1);let ids=j["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|d|d["gated"]==true).map(|d|d["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>();assert_eq!(ids.len(),2);assert_ne!(ids[0],ids[1]);
 let (code,j)=check(&p,&["--ack-security",&ids[0],"--reason","reviewed first unreachable draft insertion"]);assert_eq!(code,1);assert_eq!(j["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|d|d["gated"]==true&&d["acknowledged"]==false).count(),1);
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&ids[1],"--reason","reviewed second unreachable draft insertion"]).0,0);
}

    29	    let args=&p.args;
    30	    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    31	    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
    32	        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
    33	            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {

    51	            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
    52	        })

    69	            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
    70	        }}

    82	    }prioritized
    83	}
    84	// Preserve status/decision alternatives on each resource tuple before the

    86	fn directed_cases(p:&Program,cases:Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    87	    let identities=p.args.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Ref{..}|ir::ArgType::Principal)).map(|(n,_)|n).collect::<Vec<_>>();

   100	pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
   101	pub(super) fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
   102	    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
