   105	pub(super) fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
   106	    typed_disclosure_image(ty,data,table,key,column,physical,None)
   107	}
   108	pub(super) fn typed_disclosure_image(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value,policy:Option<&RuleExpr>)->bool{
   109	    use crate::types::Ty;
   110	    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)))&&policy.is_none_or(|p|!p.any_term(&|term|{
   111	        let column=match term{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::OldField(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c)=>Some(c.as_str()),RuleTerm::Reference{column,..}|RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{column,..}=>Some(column.as_str()),_=>None};
   112	        column.filter(|c|*c!="$security:server-clock").is_some_and(|c|data.field(c).is_err()||data.field(c).ok()!=physical.field(c).ok())
   113	    }));
   114	    match ty{
   115	        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
   116	        Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)=>typed_disclosure_image(inner,data,table,key,column,physical,policy),
   117	        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
   118	        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure_image(inner,r,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
   119	        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure_image(ty,v,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
   120	        _=>false,
   121	    }
   122	}
   123	pub(super) fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
   124	    use crate::types::Ty;
   125	    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
   126	}
   127	pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
   128	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
   129	    loop{let before=out.len();
   130	        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
   131	        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||super::super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
   132	        if out.len()==before{return out;}
   133	    }
   134	}
   135	fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
   136	    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
   137	    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
   138	}
   139	fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
   140	    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
   141	    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
   142	        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
   143	        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
   144	            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
   145	        }else{true}
   146	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   147	    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
   148	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
   149	        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
   150	        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|super::super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
   151	        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}
   152	
   153	        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
   154	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
   155	            if out.exhausted(){return;}
   156	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
   157	            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
   158	            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
   159	            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
   160	            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}
   161	
   162	            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
   163	            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
   164	            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
   165	                let cases=fixture_cases(bound,&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
   166	                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
   167	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
   168	                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
   169	                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
   170	                    out.attempted+=1;
   171	                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
   172	                        out.attempted+=1;
   173	                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
   174	                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
   175	                        }
   176	                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
   177	                        if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
   178	                        continue 'actors;
   179	                    }
   180	                }
   181	            }
   182	            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
   183	            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
   184	            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
   185	            if !reserve_observers(out,3){return;}
   186	            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   187	            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
   188	            if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world invalid: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
   189	            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
   190	            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   191	            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
   192	                let p=&bp.program;
   193	                let cases=fixture_cases(bound,p,rows,actors,out);
   194	                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
   195	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
   196	                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}
   197	                    let raw_args=args;
   198	                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&p.args,&raw_args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
   199	                    if args!=raw_args{
   200	                        if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
   201	                        absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger fork: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
   202	                        if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger absence: {why}",p.name));continue;}
   203	                        if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: ledger absence world invalid: {why}",p.name));continue;}
   204	                        if readback(&absent)!=Ok(absent_rows.clone()){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired argument ledger changes the absent base world",p.name));continue;}
   205	                    }
   206	                    out.attempted+=2;
   207	                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
   208	                    if a==b{continue;}
   209	                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: paired history observation is unstable or unavailable on repeat",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
   210	                    let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
   211	                    if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
   212	                    continue 'actors;
   213	                }
   214	            }
   215	        }}
   216	    }
   217	}
   218	fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
   219	    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
   220	}
   221	fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   222	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
   223	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   224	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
   225	            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   226	            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
   227	            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
   228	            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
   229	                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
   230	            }
   231	        }
   232	    }
   233	}
   234	fn changed(bound:&Bound,_p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   235	    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
   236	    // Disappearance is coverage evidence only. Actual delete authority is
   237	    // judged by the executed-write trace, never by a commit-end image diff.
   238	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|matches!(i.target.as_str(),"delete"|"write")){
   239	        if bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].is_expiring()&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|new|new.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
   240	            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: implicit retirement is not an executed delete; delete intent requires an authored write boundary",inv.target_label()));
   241	        }
   242	    }
   243	}
   244	
   245	fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
   246	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
   247	    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
   248	    if !ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(table,_)|!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table).is_empty()){return out;}
   249	    if !campaign.reserve(1){return Vec::new();}
   250	    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return Vec::new();};
   251	    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
   252	        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
   253	        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
   254	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
   255	        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
   256	            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
   257	            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
   258	            let mut targets=Vec::new();
   259	            for case in cases{
   260	                let mut args=case.clone();
   261	                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
   262	                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
   263	                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
   264	                }}
   265	                if columns.iter().any(|c|args.get(c).is_some_and(|v|row.field(c).ok()==Some(v))){continue;}
   266	                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
   267	            }
   268	            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
   269	            for actor in actors{
   270	                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
   271	                if !campaign.reserve(1){return out;}
   272	                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
   273	                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
   274	                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   275	            }
   276	            groups.push(row_cases);
   277	        }
   278	    }
   279	    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
   280	    out
   281	}
   282	// Prioritize actually admitted relationship changes rather than filling the
   283	// history cap with cross-products rejected by a recipient-role require.
   284	fn relationship_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
   285	    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);

 succeeded in 0ms:
   285	    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
   286	    if !p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))||!writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()&&bound.schema.tables[t].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal))){return Vec::new();}
   287	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut attempts=0;
   288	    let Ok(mut judge)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{return candidates;};
   289	    for actor in actors{
   290	        let allowed=writes.iter().any(|(t,kind)|super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()&&initial.get(t).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|{

   293	        }));
   294	        if !allowed{continue;}
   295	        for args in cases{
   296	            if attempts>=128||candidates.len()>=16||!out.reserve(1){return candidates;}attempts+=1;
   297	            let Ok(mut e)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{continue;};
   298	            let Ok(materialized)=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,args,actor)else{continue;};
   299	            if !matches!(e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,materialized).at(NOW)),Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})){continue;}
   300	            let Ok(after)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
   301	            if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|initial.get(t).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(t).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|bound.schema.tables[t].columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))){
   302	                candidates.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));
   303	            }
   304	        }
   305	    }candidates
   306	}
   307	pub(super) fn initial_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   308	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
   309	    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
   310	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   311	    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   312	    let Ok(mut engine)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return;};
   313	    observe(bound,&mut engine,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   314	}
   315	// Prefer real lifecycle successors before the general diagonal history matrix.
   316	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#163-directed-finite-histories — pruning orders finite replay, never proves safety.
   317	pub(super) fn lifecycle_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   318	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted()||bound.invariants.iter().all(|i|out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==i.site.to_string()&&v.reachability.is_reachable())){return;}
   319	    let interesting=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|{
   320	        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==i.site.to_string()&&v.reachability.is_reachable()){return false;}
   321	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&i.table];let gate=match i.target.as_str(){"read"=>super::super::columns::admission(t,i.column.as_deref()),"insert"=>t.rules.insert.clone(),"update"=>t.update_rule_judged().into_owned(),_=>RuleExpr::Allow};
   322	        i.policy.as_ref().is_none_or(|policy|kernel::implies(&gate,policy)!=Ok(true))
   323	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   324	    if interesting.is_empty(){return;}
   325	    let selected_sites=interesting.iter().map(|i|i.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   326	    let mut relevant=interesting.into_iter().flat_map(|i|std::iter::once(i.table.clone()).chain(i.policy.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies))).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   327	    for _ in 0..bound.schema.tables.len(){let old=relevant.clone();for name in &old{for kind in bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=kind.required(){relevant.insert(table.clone());}}}if old==relevant{break;}}
   328	    let has_lifecycle=relevant.iter().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..}|C::Bool|C::Principal|C::Ref{..})));
   329	    if !has_lifecycle{return;}
   330	    let transitions=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!bp.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&bp.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)).filter(|bp|{
   331	        ir::write_tables(&bp.program).iter().any(|(name,kind)|relevant.contains(name)&&(*kind!="insert"||bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|&i.table==name)))
   332	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   333	    if transitions.is_empty(){return;}
   334	    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
   335	    if !relevant.iter().any(|name|super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()&&initial.get(name).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())){
   336	        out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle needs a populated resource root; generated initialization and breadth retain bootstraps, longer empty-start histories remain uncovered".into());return;
   337	    }
   338	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   339	    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   340	    let Ok(root)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{return;};
   341	    let mut frontier=vec![(root,Vec::<Step>::new())];
   342	    fn signature(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->String{
   343	        // Equality of this projection is only an exploration heuristic. Missing
   344	        // worlds are explicitly uncovered; it never discharges an obligation.
   345	        let projected=rows.iter().map(|(name,rs)|{let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];let mut images=rs.iter().map(|r|t.columns.iter().filter(|(c,k)|c.as_str()!="id"&&matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..}|C::Bool|C::Principal|C::Ref{..})).filter_map(|(c,_)|r.field(c).ok().map(|v|(c.clone(),v.clone()))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>()).collect::<Vec<_>>();images.sort_by_key(|r|serde_json::to_string(r).unwrap());(name.clone(),images)}).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();serde_json::to_string(&projected).unwrap()
   346	    }
   347	    let mut seen=BTreeSet::from([signature(bound,&initial)]);
   348	    for _ in 0..8{
   349	        let mut next=Vec::new();let frontier_count=frontier.len();
   350	        for (base,steps) in frontier{
   351	            let Ok(before)=readback(&base)else{continue;};
   352	            for bp in &transitions{
   353	                let p=&bp.program;
   354	                let targets=ir::write_tables(p);
   355	
   356	                if targets.iter().all(|(t,kind)|*kind=="insert"&&before.get(t).is_some_and(|rs|rs.len()>=2)){out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle omits further insert-only expansion of populated tables; additional populations uncovered".into());continue;}
   357	                let ordinary=fixture_cases(bound,p,&before,&actors,out);
   358	                let mut cases=role_candidates(bound,p,&before,&actors,&ordinary,&base,out).into_iter().map(|(_,_,args)|args).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   359	                cases.extend(ordinary);let mut argument_keys=BTreeSet::new();cases.retain(|args|argument_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(args).unwrap()));
   360	                let cases=directed_cases(p,cases,out);
   361	                if cases.len()>4{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed lifecycle arguments capped at 4",p.name));}
   362	                let mut successors=Vec::<(Engine,Vec<Step>,usize,String)>::new();
   363	                let program_json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
   364	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(4){
   365	                    let mut ordered=actors.iter().map(|actor|{
   366	                        let mut score=0;let mut class=BTreeSet::new();
   367	                        for (table,rs) in &before{let roles=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);for row in rs{
   368	                            if !bound.schema.tables[table].columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&row.field(c).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone()))){continue;}
   369	                            for c in &roles{if let Ok(value)=row.field(c){class.insert(format!("{table}.{c}={value:?}"));}}
   370	                            if !roles.is_empty()&&roles.iter().any(|c|row.field(c).is_ok_and(|v|matches!(v,Value::String(role) if program_json.contains(&format!("\"String\":\"{role}\""))))){score+=4;}
   371	                            if p.args.iter().any(|(name,k)|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Ref{table:t} if t==table)&&args.get(name)==row.field("id").ok()){score+=2;class.insert(format!("owns target {table}"));}
   372	                        }}
   373	                        if program_json.contains(&format!("\"{}\"",actor)){score+=8;}
   374	                        (actor,score,class)
   375	                    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();ordered.sort_by_key(|(_,score,_)|std::cmp::Reverse(*score));
   376	                    // Two principals of one privileged role must not consume
   377	                    // both slots and hide a different admitted role's witness.
   378	                    let actor_count=ordered.len();let mut actor_classes=BTreeSet::new();ordered.retain(|(_,_,class)|actor_classes.insert(class.clone()));
   379	                    if ordered.len()<actor_count{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed role-class deduplication omits actors with matching role/target projections; actor-specific histories uncovered",p.name));}
   380	                    if ordered.len()>2{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed actor ordering keeps two candidates per argument tuple; other actors remain in breadth campaign",p.name));}
   381	                    for (viewer,_,_) in ordered.into_iter().take(2){
   382	                    if out.directed_attempts>=2048||!out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle successors capped at 2048 attempts/shared campaign budget; remaining histories uncovered".into());return;}out.directed_attempts+=1;
   383	                    let Ok(mut engine)=base.fork_fixture()else{continue;};
   384	                    let Ok(args)=materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,&args,viewer)else{continue;};
   385	                    let mut prior=None;
   386	                    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){return;}prior=base.fork_fixture().ok();}
   387	                    out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   388	                    let trace=super::executed::trace(bound,p);
   389	                    let Ok(result)=engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW).with_fixture_trace(trace.clone()))else{continue;};
   390	                    if !matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..}){out.refused+=1;continue;}
   391	                    let Ok(after)=readback(&engine)else{continue;};
   392	                    let mut history=steps.clone();history.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   393	                    super::executed::record(bound,p,&trace,&result.state,&after,&initial,viewer,&history,&mut engine,out);
   394	                    changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&history,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);
   395	                    if selected_sites.iter().all(|site|out.violations.iter().any(|v|&v.invariant_site==site&&v.reachability.is_reachable())){
   396	                        out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle stopped after every selected independent intent had a replayed violation; additional histories uncovered".into());return;
   397	                    }
   398	                    if before==after{continue;}
   399	                    out.sequences+=1;
   400	                    if !seen.insert(signature(bound,&after)){out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle signature merges worlds differing in non-status payload; remaining worlds uncovered".into());continue;}
   401	                    observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&history,out);
   402	                    column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&history,out);
   403	                    if selected_sites.iter().all(|site|out.violations.iter().any(|v|&v.invariant_site==site&&v.reachability.is_reachable())){
   404	                        out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle stopped after every selected independent intent had a replayed violation; additional histories uncovered".into());return;
   405	                    }
   406	                    let progress=after.iter().filter(|(name,_)|relevant.contains(*name)).map(|(name,rs)|{let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];rs.iter().map(|r|t.columns.iter().map(|(c,k)|match k.required(){C::Enum{..} if !super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).contains(c)=>before.get(name).into_iter().flatten().find(|old|old.field("id").ok()==r.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|old|old.field(c).ok()!=r.field(c).ok()) as usize*4,C::Bool=>matches!(r.field(c),Ok(Value::Bool(true))) as usize,_=>0}).sum::<usize>()).sum::<usize>()}).sum::<usize>();
   407	                    let state_key=signature(bound,&after.iter().filter(|(name,_)|relevant.contains(*name)).map(|(name,rs)|(name.clone(),rs.clone())).collect());
   408	                    if successors.iter().any(|(_,_,_,key)|key==&state_key){out.unsupported.insert("directed successor diversity merges audit-only attribution variants; additional histories uncovered".into());continue;}
   409	                    successors.push((engine,history,progress,state_key));
   410	                    out.directed_peak_worlds=out.directed_peak_worlds.max(frontier_count+next.len()+successors.len()+1);
   411	                    successors.sort_by_key(|(_,_,priority,_)|std::cmp::Reverse(*priority));
   412	                    if successors.len()>2{successors.truncate(2);out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed successor selection keeps two priority resource states per operation/world; other states uncovered",p.name));}
   413	                }}
   414	                for (engine,history,priority,_) in successors{
   415	                    next.push((engine,history,priority));
   416	                    out.directed_peak_worlds=out.directed_peak_worlds.max(frontier_count+next.len()+1);
   417	                    next.sort_by_key(|(_,_,priority)|std::cmp::Reverse(*priority));
   418	                    if next.len()>4{next.truncate(4);out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle beam keeps four priority worlds; displaced worlds dropped immediately and remain uncovered".into());}
   419	                }
   420	            }
   421	        }
   422	        if next.is_empty(){return;}
   423	        frontier=next.into_iter().map(|(e,h,_)|(e,h)).collect();
   424	    }
   425	    out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle traces bounded to eight authored mutations; longer traces uncovered".into());
   426	}
   427	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   428	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
   429	    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   430	    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
   431	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   432	    let probes=Vec::new();
   433	    let mut groups=Vec::new();
   434	    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)){
   435	        let cases=fixture_cases(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,out);
   436	        let mut group=relationship_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out);
   437	        group.extend(role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out));
   438	        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
   439	        let mut seen=group.iter().map(|(_,viewer,args)|serde_json::to_string(&(viewer,args)).unwrap()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   440	        // Give every actor the same first argument tuple before diagonal
   441	        // variation; a referee must not see only the overturned bout by chance.
   442	        if let Some(args)=cases.first(){for actor in &actors{
   443	            if group.len()>=LIMIT||out.exhausted(){break;}
   444	            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   445	        }}
   446	        'diagonal:for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
   447	            if cases.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){break 'diagonal;}
   448	            if group.len()>=LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history candidate actors/arguments capped at 512",bp.program.name));break 'diagonal;}
   449	            let actor=&actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()];let args=&cases[n%cases.len()];
   450	            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   451	        }}
   452	        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
   453	    }
   454	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut candidate_keys=BTreeSet::new();
   455	    // Front-load a small fair frontier of role and relationship changes so
   456	    // ordinary no-op/rejected mutations cannot consume all observer work first.
   457	    for group in &groups {
   458	        let p=group[0].0;
   459	        let priority=ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,kind)|*kind!="insert"&&(!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()||p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))));
   460	        if priority{for c in group.iter().take(8){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}}}}
   461	        if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}
   462	    }
   463	    'candidates:for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());}if candidates.len()==LIMIT{break 'candidates;}}}}
   464	    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   465	    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   466	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
   467	        for row in initial.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){for viewer in &actors{
   468	            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   469	            if policy_in(bound,inv,&initial,viewer,None,row,&mut base)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   470	            let mut witness=Campaign::default();violation(&mut witness,inv,&[],viewer,row,None,&initial,"always");
   471	            if let Some(mut v)=witness.violations.pop(){v.reachability=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace;v.operation="$fixture.assertion".into();v.rule_path="initial trusted fixture assertion; no authored mutation".into();v.scope="definite violation in authored fixture; initialization trusted, no authored transition claimed".into();out.violations.push(v);}break;
   472	        }}
   473	    }
   474	    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   475	    // Spend half the pair budget on grant changes followed by an operation
   476	    // as the affected principal; interleave first steps and successor names.
   477	    let mut role_groups=Vec::new();
   478	    for (i,(p,_,args)) in candidates.iter().enumerate().take(LIMIT){
   479	        let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
   480	        let rosters=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter_map(|(t,_)|{
   481	            let roles=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&t);
   482	            roles.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))).then_some(t)
   483	        }).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   484	        if rosters.is_empty(){continue;}
   485	        let mut recipients=BTreeSet::new();
   486	        for (name,kind) in &p.args {if let Some(value)=args.get(name){match kind {
   487	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>{if let Value::Id(actor)=value{recipients.insert(actor.clone());}},
   488	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rosters.contains(table)=>{
   489	                if let Some(row)=initial.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(value)){
   490	                    for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(actor))=row.field(column){recipients.insert(actor.clone());}}
   491	                }
   492	            },_=>{}
   493	        }}}
   494	        let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
   495	        let successors=candidates.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(j,(next,actor,_))|(next.name!=p.name&&recipients.contains(actor)&&seen.insert((next.name.clone(),actor.clone()))).then_some((i,j))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   496	        if !successors.is_empty(){role_groups.push(successors);}
   497	    }
   498	    let mut role_pairs=Vec::new();
   499	    'pairs:for n in 0..role_groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &role_groups{if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){role_pairs.push(*pair);if role_pairs.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'pairs;}}}}
   500	    // Breadth first, then targeted and diagonal pairs: no first step owns the cap.
   501	    let history_limit=if out.history_limit==0{LIMIT}else{out.history_limit.min(LIMIT)};
   502	    let singles=candidates.len().min(history_limit);
   503	    let mut setups=Vec::new();let mut role_changes=Vec::new();let mut committed=Vec::new();
   504	    let mut setup_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut role_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut pair_plan=Vec::new();
   505	    for n in 0..singles+history_limit {
   506	        if !out.reserve(1){break;}
   507	        if n==singles&&!candidates.is_empty(){
   508	            let disclosures=super::executed::pairs(bound,&candidates,&committed);
   509	            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
   510	            for pair in disclosures.into_iter().take(LIMIT/2){if seen.insert(pair){pair_plan.push(pair);}}
   511	            'setup:for &second in &role_changes{for &first in &setups{if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'setup;}}}}
   512	            for &(first,second) in &role_pairs{if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT{break;}if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));}}
   513	            let mut k=0;
   514	            while pair_plan.len()<LIMIT{pair_plan.push((k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()));k+=1;}
   515	        }
   516	        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let (first,second)=pair_plan[n-singles];vec![first,second]};
   517	        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   518	        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
   519	        for i in indices{
   520	            if !out.reserve(1){break;}
   521	            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   522	            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
   523	            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){break;}match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
   524	            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   525	            let trace=super::executed::trace(bound,p);
   526	            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW).with_fixture_trace(trace.clone())){
   527	                Ok(result)=>{
   528	                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
   529	                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   530	                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   531	                    if sent&&n<singles{
   532	                        if before!=after{
   533	                            let transition=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(name,t)|before.get(name).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(name).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|t.columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..})&&old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok()))));
   534	                            if transition{committed.insert(0,i);}else{committed.push(i);}
   535	                        }
   536	                        let signature=serde_json::to_string(&(&p.name,viewer,&candidates[i].2)).unwrap();
   537	                        let new_media=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"&&initial.get(&inv.table).is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)).any(|inv|{
   538	                            let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
   539	                            after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|table.columns.iter().any(|(column,kind)|kind.contains_asset()&&row.field(column).is_ok_and(|v|*v!=Value::Null&&!matches!(v,Value::Array(xs) if xs.is_empty()))))
   540	                        });
   541	                        if new_media&&setup_seen.insert(signature.clone()){setups.push(i);}
   542	                        let changed_role=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,_)|{
   543	                            let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
   544	                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))
   545	                        });
   546	                        if changed_role&&role_seen.insert(signature){role_changes.push(i);}
   547	                    }
   548	                    if sent{super::executed::record(bound,p,&trace,&result.state,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut engine,out);changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
   549	                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
   550	                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
   551	            }
   552	        }
   553	    }
   554	    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
   555	}
   556	
   557	#[cfg(test)] mod directed_pruning_tests{use super::*;
   558	#[test] fn correlated_boolean_arguments_are_explicitly_uncovered(){
   559	 let bound=crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),"table docs:\n  x: bool\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(a: bool,b: bool):\n  return insert docs {x:a and b}\n".into())]);
   560	 assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bound.diagnostics);let p=&bound.programs[0].program;
   561	 let cases=[(false,false),(false,true),(true,false),(true,true)].into_iter().map(|(a,b)|BTreeMap::from([("a".into(),Value::Bool(a)),("b".into(),Value::Bool(b))])).collect();
   562	 let mut out=Campaign::default();let selected=directed_cases(p,cases,&mut out);assert_eq!(selected.len(),3);assert!(out.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("argument grouping")));
   563	}}
 let own=include_str!("security/wave23/042.q");let s=format!("{}\ninvariant signings.agencyBillingNotes write only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']\n",own.lines().filter(|l|!l.trim_start().starts_with("invariant ")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n"));let b=bound(&s);let mut c=Campaign::default();challenges::generated_campaign(&b,&mut c,false);
 assert!(c.violations.iter().any(|v|v.reachability.is_reachable()&&v.operation=="createSigning"&&v.history.last().and_then(|s|s.args.get("agencyBillingNotes")).is_some_and(|v|*v!=snapback4_core::Value::Null)&&v.row.field("agencyBillingNotes").is_ok_and(|v|*v!=snapback4_core::Value::Null)),"no non-null replay among {} witnesses",c.violations.len());
 assert!(c.directed_attempts<=2048&&c.directed_peak_worlds<=16);
}
#[test]fn changed_field_guards_survive_a_helper_and_lexical_aliases(){
 let s="table roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('owner','writer')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ntable docs:\n  secret: text <=100 ? read <- exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner']\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(secret:text <=100 ?):\n  return insert docs {secret}\nfn editHelper(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  old = docs[doc] ! else MISSING\n  next = secret\n  if next != old.secret:\n    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  update docs[doc] {secret:next}\n  return null\nmutation edit(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  return editHelper(doc,secret)\n";
 assert!(has(s,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let equality=s.replace("if next != old.secret:\n    require", "if next = old.secret:\n    require true else SAME\n  else:\n    require");assert!(has(&equality,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let reassigned=s.replace("    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  update", "    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  else:\n    next = 'changed'\n  update");assert!(!has(&reassigned,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let multiple=s.replace("  if next != old.secret:", "  update docs[doc] {secret:'intervening'}\n  if next != old.secret:");assert!(!has(&multiple,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
}
#[test]fn effectful_loop_domains_never_reuse_a_preloop_column_guard(){
 let s="table roles:\n  principal:principal\n  role:enum('owner','writer')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\ntable docs:\n  secret:text <=100 ? read <- exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner']\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(secret:text <=100 ?):\n  return insert docs {secret}\nfn acquire():\n  upsert roles[viewer] {principal:viewer,role:'owner'}\n  return [1]\nmutation edit(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  old = docs[doc] ! else MISSING\n  if secret != old.secret:\n    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  for n in acquire():\n    update docs[doc] {secret}\n  return null\n";
 let rejected=compile(&[("schema.q".into(),s.into())]);assert!(rejected.diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.code=="E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT"),"the original helper-list counterexample is already outside the language");
 let bounded=s.replace("mutation edit(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):", "mutation edit(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?, items:[int] <=4):").replace("for n in acquire():", "for n in items:");assert!(!has(&bounded,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
}
#[test]fn module_local_editors_do_not_merge_their_guard_paths(){
 let tables="table roles:\n  principal:principal\n  role:enum('owner','writer')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ntable docs:\n  secret:text <=100 ? read <- exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner']\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(secret:text <=100 ?):\n  return insert docs {secret}\n";
 let guarded="mutation edit(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  old = docs[doc] ! else MISSING\n  if secret != old.secret:\n    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  update docs[doc] {secret}\n";
 let open="mutation edit(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  update docs[doc] {secret}\n";
 let sources=vec![("schema.q".into(),tables.into()),("a.q".into(),guarded.into()),("b.q".into(),open.into())];let b=compile(&sources);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);let mut r=security::analyze_rules(&b);security::add_source_notes(&mut r,&sources,&b);assert!(r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"&&n.message.contains("a.edit")));
}
#[test]fn effectful_requirements_and_conditions_do_not_become_stable_edit_guards(){
 let s="table roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('owner','writer')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\ntable docs:\n  secret: text <=100 ? read <- exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner']\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(secret:text <=100 ?):\n  return insert docs {secret}\nfn acquire():\n  upsert roles[viewer] {principal:viewer,role:'owner'}\n  return true\nmutation edit(doc:docs,secret:text <=100 ?):\n  old = docs[doc] ! else MISSING\n  if secret != old.secret:\n    require acquire() else ACQUIRE\n    require exists roles[viewer,role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  update docs[doc] {secret}\n  return null\n";
 assert!(!has(s,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let condition=s.replace("    require acquire() else ACQUIRE\n", "").replace("  update docs[doc] {secret}", "  if acquire():\n    update docs[doc] {secret}");assert!(!has(&condition,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
}

snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/intent_walk.rs:4:pub(crate) fn expression(value: &mut Expr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
//! Mutable syntax traversal shared by planning and final index-name resolution.
use crate::ast::*;

pub(crate) fn expression(value: &mut Expr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    match value {
        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => read_children(read, visit),
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { expression(left, visit); expression(right, visit); }
        Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => { for item in items { expression(item, visit); } }
        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => { for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } } }
        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => { expression(base, visit); shape(fields, visit); }
        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => { expression(then, visit); expression(condition, visit); expression(otherwise, visit); }
        Expr::Walk { seed, .. } => expression(seed, visit),
        Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => {
            for key in keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
            for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } }
            if let Some(value) = condition { expression(value, visit); }
        }
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            for value in [&mut merge.matching, &mut merge.filter, &mut merge.cursor].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
            let mut lanes = Vec::new();
            for mut lane in std::mem::take(&mut merge.lanes) {
                if let Some(predicate) = &mut lane.admission { expression(predicate, visit); }
                if let Some((_, read)) = &mut lane.nulls {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    let Expr::Read(planned) = value else { unreachable!("planned null scan") };
                    **read = planned;
                }
                if let Some(value) = &mut lane.distinct { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(fields) = &mut lane.project { shape(fields, visit); }
                for value in &mut lane.prefix { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some((_, value)) = &mut lane.loop_ { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(read) = &mut lane.planned {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    match value {
                        Expr::Read(planned) => **read = planned,
                        Expr::Merge(nested) if lane.loop_.is_none() => {
                            for mut child in nested.lanes {
                                child.branch = lane.branch.clone();
                                child.project = lane.project.clone();
                                lanes.push(child);
                            }
                            continue;
                        }
                        _ => unreachable!("a planned lane is a read or an expanded read"),
                    }
                }
                lanes.push(lane);
            }
            merge.lanes = lanes;
        }
        Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => {}
    }
    visit(value);
}

fn read_children(read: &mut ReadExpr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for key in &mut read.keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
    for seek in &mut read.seeks { expression(&mut seek.value, visit); }
    for value in [&mut read.filter, &mut read.matching].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
    if let Some(value) = read.range.as_mut().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_mut()) { expression(value, visit); }
}

pub(super) fn shape(fields: &mut [ShapeField], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for field in fields { if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { expression(value, visit); } }
}

pub(super) fn statements(body: &mut [Stmt], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for stmt in body {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Bind { value, .. } | Stmt::Expr { value, .. } | Stmt::Return { value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition, visit),
            Stmt::Emit { payload, once, .. } => {
                expression(payload, visit);
                if let Some(key) = once { expression(key, visit); }
            }
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body, .. } => { expression(condition, visit); statements(then_body, visit); statements(else_body, visit); }
            Stmt::As { principal: domain, body, .. } | Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => { expression(domain, visit); statements(body, visit); }
            Stmt::Break { .. } | Stmt::Continue { .. } => {}
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn document(doc: &mut Document, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — historical lookups receive the same predicate/index planning as ordinary reads.
    for fill in doc.backfills.iter_mut().chain(&mut doc.downfills) {expression(&mut fill.value,visit);}

    for program in &mut doc.programs { statements(&mut program.prelude, visit); statements(&mut program.body, visit); }
    for function in &mut doc.functions {
        match &mut function.body { FunctionBody::Expression(value) => expression(value, visit), FunctionBody::Block(body) => statements(body, visit) }
    }
    for decl in &mut doc.shapes { shape(&mut decl.fields, visit); }
}
//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
}
pub(super) fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}
pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    }out
}
pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
}

//! Explain residual write tests without lowering them to a private pre-read.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    pub(super) fn extra_write_test(&self, info: &'a TableInfo, write: &A, original: Diagnostic) -> Diagnostic {
        let A::Write { table, keys, site, .. } = write else { return original };
        let read = ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table: table.clone(),
            keys: keys.clone(), index: None, range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() };
        let Ok(read) = super::super::mixed_keys::read(info, &read, self.catalog) else { return original };
        let read = read.into_owned();
        let keys = &read.keys;
        // Speculate only for an explanation. Mistyped, ambiguous or incomplete
        // keys keep their original error; no setup escapes into the program.
        let mut probe = self.clone();
        if probe.read_value(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint, &mut setup::Body::default()).is_err() { return original; }
        let Ok((_, index, _)) = probe.select_index(info, &read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint, &mut setup::Body::default()) else { return original };
        if keys.len() <= index.columns.len() { return original; }
        let positional = !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(table.clone(), index.name.clone()))
            && info.indexes.iter().filter(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.columns.len() == index.columns.len()
                && !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(table.clone(), candidate.name.clone()))).count() == 1;
        let key: Vec<_> = index.columns.iter().map(|column| {
            let mut part = keys.iter().find(|part| part.column.as_ref() == Some(column)).expect("validated named key").clone();
            if positional { part.column = None; }
            part
        }).collect();
        let mut checked = read.clone();
        checked.keys = key.clone();
        checked.keys.extend(keys.iter().filter(|part| !index.columns.contains(part.column.as_ref().expect("named key"))).cloned());
        let mut fixed = write.clone();
        let A::Write { keys, .. } = &mut fixed else { unreachable!() };
        *keys = key;
        Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_WRITE_TESTS", "a write target names one row by its key; extra column tests belong in an explicit require",
            format!("require exists {} else NOT_ADMIN\n{}", print::read_expr(&checked), print::expression(&fixed))).guide("writes")
    }
}

//! Authored write boundaries and compiler-typed committed mutation payloads.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#151-every-executed-write — no net-image inference for intermediate writes.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::engine::fixture_trace::{Trace,Intent};
pub(super) fn trace(bound:&Bound,p:&Program)->Trace {
    Trace::new(bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|!matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")&&inv.operation.as_ref().is_none_or(|op|op==&p.name)).filter_map(|inv|Some(Intent{
        site:inv.site.to_string(),table:inv.table.clone(),column:inv.column.clone(),policy:inv.policy.clone()?,
        effects:super::super::effects(inv).iter().map(|word|super::super::effect(word)).collect(),
    })).collect())
}
pub(super) fn record(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,trace:&Trace,result:&snapback4_core::WriteState,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[histories::Step],engine:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for observation in trace.take(){
        let Some(inv)=bound.invariants.iter().find(|inv|inv.site.to_string()==observation.site)else{continue;};
        match observation.allowed{
            Some(false)=>if let Some(row)=observation.next.as_ref().or(observation.old.as_ref()){
                if histories::violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,observation.old.as_ref(),initial,match observation.effect{Effect::Insert=>"insert",Effect::Update=>"update",Effect::Delete=>"delete",Effect::Read=>"read"}){
                    out.violations.last_mut().unwrap().rule_path="actual executed write boundary; independent policy false in the provisional transaction; enclosing authored mutation committed".into();
                }
            },
            None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: executed policy unknown; no refutation",p.name,inv.target_label()));},_=>{},
        }
    }
    let snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result:data,..}=result else{return;};
    let Some(bp)=bound.programs.iter().find(|bp|bp.program.name==p.name)else{return;};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"){
        let Some(policy)=inv.policy.as_ref()else{continue;};
        let deps=stability::dependencies(bound,policy);
        let implicit=stability::implicit(bound,p);
        if implicit.contains(&inv.table)||implicit.iter().any(|t|deps.contains(t))||ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: mutation-result policy changes require a disclosure-boundary reduction",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;
        }
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if !histories::typed_disclosure(&bp.result,data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){continue;}
            if !histories::typed_disclosure_image(&bp.result,data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row,Some(policy)){
                out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: returned policy image differs from committed image; disclosure boundary uncovered",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;
            }
            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,None,row,engine)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            if histories::violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"mutation result"){
                let v=out.violations.last_mut().unwrap();v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=steps.last().map(|s|s.args.clone()).unwrap_or_default();v.rule_path="authored mutation returned a compiler-typed protected row/column payload; stable current policy false".into();
            }
        }
    }
}

// Follow an actually admitted state change with payload-bearing operations on
// the same referenced row. This finds lifecycle-to-mutation observations without
// spending pair slots on unrelated roster and creation combinations.
pub(super) fn pairs(bound:&Bound,candidates:&[(&Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)],committed:&[usize])->Vec<(usize,usize)>{
    let protected=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read").map(|i|i.table.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut groups:BTreeMap<(String,String),Vec<(usize,usize)>>=BTreeMap::new();let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
    for (second,(p,actor,args)) in candidates.iter().enumerate(){
        let Some(bp)=bound.programs.iter().find(|bp|bp.program.name==p.name)else{continue;};
        for &first in committed{
            let (before,who,inputs)=&candidates[first];if before.name==p.name{continue;}
            let shared=protected.iter().any(|table|histories::typed_source(&bp.result,table)&&ir::write_tables(before).iter().any(|(t,_)|t==table)&&before.args.iter().any(|(a,k)|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Ref{table:t} if t==table)&&p.args.iter().any(|(b,k)|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Ref{table:t} if t==table)&&inputs.get(a)==args.get(b))));
            if shared&&seen.insert((before.name.clone(),who.clone(),p.name.clone(),actor.clone())){groups.entry((p.name.clone(),actor.clone())).or_default().push((first,second));}
        }
    }
    let mut out=Vec::new();for n in 0..groups.values().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in groups.values(){if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){out.push(*pair);if out.len()==256{return out;}}}}out
}

// Identify direct argument supplies to declared column-write intent. This only
// prioritizes proposals; actual interpreter admission still decides every case.
pub(super) fn write_arguments(bound:&Bound,p:&Program)->BTreeSet<String>{
    fn walk(v:&serde_json::Value,f:&mut impl FnMut(&serde_json::Value)){
        f(v);match v{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){walk(x,f)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{walk(x,f)},_=>{}}
    }
    let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();let mut bindings=BTreeMap::new();
    walk(&json,&mut |v|for tag in ["Const","Let"]{if let Some(b)=v.get(tag)&&let(Some(n),Some(e))=(b.get("name").and_then(|v|v.as_str()),b.get("value")){bindings.insert(n.to_string(),e.clone());}});
    fn resolve<'a>(v:&'a serde_json::Value,bindings:&'a BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,depth:usize)->&'a serde_json::Value{
        if depth>32{return v;}if let Some(n)=v.get("Variable").and_then(|v|v.as_str())&&let Some(e)=bindings.get(n){resolve(e,bindings,depth+1)}else{v}
    }
    let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    walk(&json,&mut |v|for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
        let Some(w)=v.get(tag)else{continue;};let table=w.get("table").or_else(||w.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(|v|v.as_str());
        let Some(payload)=w.get("row").or_else(||w.get("patch"))else{continue;};let payload=resolve(payload,&bindings,0);
        let Some(fields)=payload.get("Object").and_then(|v|v.as_object())else{continue;};
        for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|Some(i.table.as_str())==table&&i.column.is_some()&&!matches!(i.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")&&i.operation.as_ref().is_none_or(|op|op==&p.name)){
            if let Some(value)=fields.get(inv.column.as_ref().unwrap())&&let Some(arg)=resolve(value,&bindings,0).get("Variable").and_then(|v|v.as_str())&&p.args.contains_key(arg){out.insert(arg.into());}
        }
    });out
}

            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        Ok(value)
    }

    pub(super) fn parse_read(&mut self, table: &str, site: &Site) -> Fallible<ReadExpr> {
        self.take("[")?;
        let mut keys = Vec::new();
        let mut seeks = Vec::new();
        while !self.at("]") {
            let key_site = self.peek().site.clone();
            let mut column = None;
            let mut end = 1;
            if self.at_kind(Kind::Word) {
                while self.peek_at(end).value == "." && self.peek_at(end + 1).kind == Kind::Word { end += 2; }
            }
            if self.at_kind(Kind::Word) && self.peek_at(end).value == "!=" {
                return Err(syntax_with(&key_site,
                    "probe brackets take named equalities, not != tests",
                    "pin a full unique key and compare the row field outside the brackets, or use a bounded program `exists table[key] where .column != value`; program probes see only caller-readable rows, while table-rule probes see all rows; use unique/cap/after insert constraints for invariants across hidden rows"));
            }
            if self.at_kind(Kind::Word) && self.peek_at(end).value == "=" {
                column = Some(self.maintain_column()?);
                self.take("=")?;
            }
            if column.is_none() && self.at_kind(Kind::Word) && matches!(self.peek_at(end).value.as_str(), ">" | ">=" | "<" | "<=") {
                let column = self.maintain_column()?;
                let op = self.peek().value.clone();
                self.position += 1;
                let value = self.expression(0, &[",", "]"])?;
                seeks.push(SeekBound { column, op, value, site: key_site });
            } else {
                if !seeks.is_empty() { return Err(syntax_with(&key_site, "pin the prefix before the seek comparisons", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index")); }
                let value = self.expression(0, &[",", "]"])?;
                keys.push(KeyPart { column, value, site: key_site });
            }
            if !self.maybe(",") {
                break;
            }
        }
        self.take("]")?;
        Ok(ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table: table.to_owned(), keys, index: None, range: None, filter: None, matching: None, seeks, site: site.clone() })
    }

                }

                let (then_out, then_bindings) = self.branch(condition, true, then_body, completes)?;
                let (else_out, else_bindings) = self.branch(condition, false, else_body, completes)?;
                let then_flow = flow::Outcomes::block(&then_out);
                let else_flow = flow::Outcomes::block(&else_out);
                // Only continuing paths meet at the join. Branch locals do
                // not escape, but a fact proved by every survivor does.
                for (name, mut binding) in before {
                    let then_ty = if then_bindings[&name].runtime == binding.runtime { &then_bindings[&name].ty } else { &binding.ty };
                    let else_ty = if else_bindings[&name].runtime == binding.runtime { &else_bindings[&name].ty } else { &binding.ty };
                    binding.ty = match (then_flow.exits(), else_flow.exits()) {
                        (false, false) => self.join(then_ty, else_ty, &joins::local(&name, site), &joins::local(&name, site))?,
                        (false, true) => then_ty.clone(),
                        (true, false) => else_ty.clone(),
                        (true, true) => binding.ty.clone(),

                    binding.fields = match (then_flow.exits(), else_flow.exits()) {

                        (false, true) => then_fields.clone(),
                        (true, false) => else_fields.clone(),
                        (true, true) => binding.fields.clone(),
                    };
                    // Break/continue still carry declarations to the loop's
                    // fixed point; return/refusal carry none to any later use.
                    binding.declared = match (then_flow.terminates(), else_flow.terminates()) {
                        (false, false) => self.join(&then_bindings[&name].declared, &else_bindings[&name].declared, &joins::local(&name, site), &joins::local(&name, site))?,
                        (false, true) => then_bindings[&name].declared.clone(),
                        (true, false) => else_bindings[&name].declared.clone(),
                        (true, true) => binding.declared.clone(),
                    };

                        if !binding.origins.iter().any(|(_, source)| *source == origin.1) { binding.origins.push(origin.clone()); }
                    }
                    binding.revision = then_bindings[&name].revision.max(else_bindings[&name].revision);
                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, binding);
                }
                out.push_if(expr, then_out, else_out);
                Ok(())
            }

//! Index choice and optional tuning. Examples compile in tests/guide.rs.
pub(super) const CARD: &str = r##"# Indexes: chosen from the read

Indexes let reads seek the rows that match a predicate in the requested order.
The compiler chooses or creates them from your reads and declarations.

```quarry
table tasks:
  ownerId: principal
  projectId: id
  done: bool
  at: time
  unique ownerId, projectId else PROJECT_EXISTS 'This project already exists.'
  read <- .ownerId = viewer
  insert <- .ownerId = viewer
  update <- .ownerId = viewer
  online only
query one(projectId: id):
  return tasks[viewer, projectId]
query open(c: cursor ?):
  return tasks where .ownerId = viewer and .done = false first 20 by .at after c
```

`by .id` sorts ids, not creation times. `last N` reverses order: newest-first with chronological keys such as `.at`.

State the predicate and order. `check` chooses an existing index or creates
one. Rules, sync, caps and expiry also obtain their access indexes. A table
needs no `by` declaration for these reads.

`unique ownerId, projectId` is a product constraint: one row per pair.
`tasks[x]` selects identity when `x` is typed `id` or `tasks`; otherwise
exactly one declared unique key must accept the argument types in order.
Name columns when several keys accept those types: `tasks[ownerId = viewer,
projectId = projectId]`. Synthesized indexes never choose positional meaning.
In either key spelling (also `exists`), arguments for nullable columns of the
selected unique key must be provably non-null: use required types or `if x != null` guards
(`x != null and exists ...` in rules). Otherwise `E_QUARRY_NULL_KEY` refuses
the ambiguous null ties across replicas. Guard nullable arguments before
`require exists`: it proves row presence, not optional field presence.
To read null matches, use a bounded
`where` population ordered `by .id`, or address a row by id. Null for a
unique key argument returns no row at runtime, without scanning ties; the
guard is still required at compile time; maintained aggregate null groups are unchanged.
A fully pinned maintained group is unique even
with null, including rule probes that also test its value.
E_QUARRY_POINT_KEY refuses a point probe whose key can match several rows.
A fully pinned superset of a required unique key counts as unique too.
Use a unique key or a bounded `where` population; its scalar probe reports unavailable
when a scan is cut. Rule probes require a unique key.
A visible unique collision uses the optional `else CODE 'message'` hook;
a hidden collision keeps its ordinary authorization refusal. `if absent`
returns null on a visible clash. Codes are UPPER_SNAKE and never start with E_.

A bound row addresses itself: after `s = slots[slot] ! else NOT_FOUND`,
`slots[s]` reads, updates and deletes that row, as `slots[s.id]` does.

## Small lookup tables: `cap N`

A table that stays small (categories, plans, tags) declares `cap N` with no
`by`: at most N rows in the whole table. Every query may then read it whole,
filtered or not, with no `first N` and no cursor:

```quarry
table categories:
  name: text 1..40
  unique name else CATEGORY_EXISTS
  public 'shop categories'
  insert <- allow
  cap 50
  sync public
query categoryList():
  return categories by .name
mutation addCategory(name: text 1..40):
  return insert categories { name }
```

The 51st insert refuses `E_CONSTRAINT: categories holds at most 50 rows`.
A device predicts it when it holds the whole table (`sync public` with no
horizon); a private partition, a horizon or an expiring table queues the write for the server. A cap bounds a read only
up to the 8,192-row examined ceiling, and other meters still apply. Adding or
lowering a cap on a populated table checks the stored rows (`E_ADOPT`);
raising one applies in place. On an expiring table the cap counts live rows.
A table that can grow pages with `first N` and a cursor instead.

## Private lists: pin the audience

A private page is complete when its planned ranges decide the read rule.
`N_NEVER_COMPLETE` prints the missing source edit for unsupported shapes.
The planner supplies party, membership and positive compound ranges, including
owner/public/member lists. An exact positive `where exists authors[.id, viewer]`

Apply the media non-null requirement only to the direct-column alternative; independently recognize the exact parent obligation plus nullable child-skipping branch. Add this nullable-child regression.

This is a missing review obligation, not a false safety verdict: independent replay may still refute the parent invariant. Static review only; no edits, builds, or tests performed.
